fork1 changelog (newest first). Base: v19.8 (stable, md5 7cd44b01cad22c95a9fbd877469fa9d2).

f1.337 (2026-11-05, branch f1337) - pre-release blocker review fixes (no feature changes).
  - The author: review for release blockers only before posting (4 subagents: core/input/seats/settings, passes/menus/camera,
    pip_hud, enemies/marker; each finding checked again). Fixed:
    #1 MakeInlineHook(.., outOrig): the original is published BEFORE the jump is written - the PlayStation HID reader hook
       runs on its own thread and could be entered with Orig_HidRead still null right after the other threads resumed
       (a start-up crash with a DS4 / DualSense plugged in; never seen - no PS pad in the logs). Also ProcessEvent and the
       three key hooks.
    #2 ProcessEvent not hookable (another tool hooked it first, e.g. UE4SS): Orig_ProcessEvent = the real entry (was null ->
       the first frame's pause check crashed).
    #3 a partial helper-hook failure (co-op off) left GetAxisPair hooked with a null original - set as each hook goes live.
    #4 co-op switched itself off (another game build): every XInput export passes straight through (was still filtered:
       slots reported connected-but-idle - a DirectInput pad could stop working).
    #5 HUD copy: root widget / message log candidates are checked to still be that class before IsVisible / the rect call
       (the class caches are keyed by address - a reused address could make a non-widget pass).
    #6 summon gauges: their classes kept as weak references (were raw pointers kept forever).
    #7 boss target log lines use the stored name, not the possibly stale enemy pointer (f1.234-type NameOf crash); every
       entry stores its name.
  - Left for after the release (minor, logged in the results doc): a made Cancel's B key-up in players 2/3's menus, the
    controller pointer check in PcMoveSwap, raw row/slot pointers of the party-row gap fix, the boss entry reuse by a new
    enemy at the same unit address, the phase guard's retarget in solo / Vanilla, g_Bp[4] size, the 1 s stale unit window.

    the mod" (it is: the .pyw is in both zips; tools/build_exe.sh in the source zip). Review (subagent) -> fixed:
    #1 the ini keeps its own encoding (UTF-16 LE/BE, UTF-8 +/- BOM, else cp1252; was latin-1 in / ASCII "?" out - a UTF-16
       file was shown as defaults and mangled on Save); lines split only on CRLF / LF.
    #2 safe save: temporary file + os.replace (direct write as the fallback); .bak via copyfile without the read-only flag.
    #3 a fresh Save with no file and no change writes nothing (the mod writes its commented default file); a warning when
       xinput1_3.dll isn't next to the settings file.
    #4 every number box is range-checked (CommandMenuScale*, PartyPanelScale*, MessageLogX/Y were not) + whole numbers where
       the mod reads IniInt. #5 metre boxes accept "2,6" / "2.6 m"; bad text is reported on Save.
    #6 Co-op Balanced = the real default: [Balance3] Player1-3Rates 85 (DLL: a missing key = 85 unless the shared [Balance]
       key exists; default ini; app SET_DEFAULTS).
    #7 thirds texts say "with Multiple monitors / ultrawide on". #8 no fixed D:\ path - the game folder is found through
       Steam (registry SteamPath + libraryfolders.vdf). #9 Windows device names (CON, NUL ...) as profile names get
       " profile" added; profiles / presets saved as UTF-8. #10 a "; note" after a value survives a Save.
  - Checked under wine: his ini and the default ini load with no validation errors and save byte-identical; a UTF-16 file
    with a note edits in place and stays UTF-16.

f1.320 (2026-11-05, branch f1285) - tilted menus: the real cause + fix (MenuTiltFix=1, default).
  - the author: "How do we fix it?" / "Maybe the fix is to put the 'eye' in the middle of the screen?". RE (unicorn
    emulation of 0x14E6B75-0x14E70B6): the 3D panel matrix is x' = 2x/W - 1 + ndc_c*k*z, y' = 1 - 2y/H + ndcy_c*k*z,
    w' = 1 + k*z (W x H 1920 x 1080, k = 0.001/scale) - the perspective centre is each panel's OWN middle, taken from its
    LAYOUT position (FGeometry +0xC/+0x10, scale +8). The split screen moves players' menus with render translations (and
    render scale), so a moved menu is drawn far from the spot its tilt is centred on - P3's moved furthest, so worst.
  - 36h TiltRenderPosPatch: 4 one-byte displacement patches make those reads use the render transform (+0x2C/+0x30, M +0x1C)
    while split windows show or spanning (bytes checked first; put back otherwise). Unmoved panels: no change.
    MenuTiltFix=0 = game's own, 2 = the f1.318 research hook. Log "MENUTILT3:".

f1.319 (2026-11-05, branch f1285) - tilted-menu change OFF by default (f1.318 test: "Too large, not in the right spot").
  - f1.318 result (18:58, thirds over 2 monitors): field menu / party list and battle Tactical Mode menus too large and
    misplaced. Log: the paint's 5th argument +8..+0x14 IS a rectangle, 0,0 1920,1080 = the HUD's LAYOUT space (qwords
    ...0000043800000780), and the canvas geometry is in the same 1920x1080 units -> the game's eye = layout x 960 = screen
    x 960 while spanning (inside player 1's column) - why P1 looks right, P2 leans, P3 leans most. But changing that size
    also rescales the panel (not only the eye), nested canvases inherit the changed size (children saw 640 / 2215 / 2664),
    and 36h mapped layout units as if at the game's scale 2 (the draw hook draws them at scale 1, 0,540) - wrong parts.
  - MenuTiltFix default 0 (hook not installed at all); MenuTiltFix=1 = the f1.318 behaviour for research.
  - The f1.318 field party-list move (36b) stays.

f1.317 (2026-11-05, branch f1285) - tilted menus: f1.316's hook made safe (research, untested in game).
  - RE: 0x14E69F0 is slot 0 of the table at SEndCanvas +0x330 (= 0x4BED120; set by the constructor 0x14E4910 and two
    subclasses), the slot SWidget::Paint (0x3A0A780) calls for every widget - so it IS the paint the HUD uses, and its 5th
    argument is OutDrawElements (the element list: clipping stack at +0xA0), not a plain rectangle. The frustum reads its
    +8..+0x14 as ints (size = +0x10 - +8, +0x14 - +0xC), but the never-called sister copy 0x14E57B0 reads +0x10 of the same
    argument as an SWindow pointer - so which it is can't be settled from the code.
  - 36h: the lean is only applied when those four ints look like a rectangle (left/top 0..8191, size 256..16384 x
    144..16384, aspect 0.5..6); otherwise the panel is left as the game draws it and a MENUTILT2 line says so (first 6).
    The first 8 calls also log the argument's first 4 qwords raw. MENUTILT DIAG counts "not a rectangle".

f1.316 (2026-11-05, branch f1285) - tilted menus: the hook on the 3D path the HUD really uses (research + fix, untested).
  - The author: "Do more research on the menu display issue". f1.308's MENUTILT hook (SEndCanvas::OnPaint, main vtable
    slot 61) never ran. The FOV constant (pi/360, 0x4F919C8) has a second user: 0x14E69F0, slot 2 of SEndCanvas's second
    vtable 0x4BED110 (an interface at this+0x330 - "lea rbx, [rcx-0x330]"), the same off-centre frustum math with the
    "screen" from its 5th argument (a rectangle of ints +8..+0x14), FOV at iface +0x108, 3D switch iface +0x104.
  - 36h Hook_CanvasPaintI (that slot): for a 3D panel inside a player's part, the rectangle's right / bottom are set for the
    call so its middle is that part's middle (the eye looks from the middle of that player's own part); put back after.
    MENUTILT2 lines: the first 8 calls raw (rect, geometry, FOV) + every lean. MenuTiltFix=0 switches the lean off.

f1.200 (2026-10-26, branch review-batch on kb-hotswap - in progress, NOT built for install yet: test f1.197 -> f1.198 ->
  f1.199 first) + settings app f1.200 - the author's tooltip review (all wording changes are in the app's APP_VERSION note)
  Mod changes so far:
  - [General] JoinAtStart: "Auto" (the new default) = on; 0 = off. JoinHoldSeconds removed - the join button is always
    held 1 second. ShowTips, OwnCommandHud, GameReticleTint removed (reminder gone, command HUD always on, reticle
    accents always).
  - [Camera] SplitAim removed (the author: "get rid of this option altogether and make it whatever method we currently
    use") - player 1's split-screen camera always turns (the off-centre lens side by side); the old Slide code
    (camera moved sideways, with its own wall check) is gone from camera/38. An ini that still says SplitAim=Slide is
    ignored.
  - [Player2] / [Player3] WindowView removed (the author: same call, review row #44) - each window camera is always
    behind its player; the Main choice (facing the main camera's direction) is gone from pip_hud/30. An ini that still
    says WindowView=Main is ignored.
  - [Player2] / [Player3] WindowControlsFollowView removed (the author: "always on, remove it from settings") - while
    a player's window shows, their left stick always moves relative to their window camera.
  - [PictureInPicture] DynamicTargetFps default 55 (was 60) - the author: "60 is a common in game setting" (a target
    equal to the game's limit leaves no room to step back up). An ini that already has the key keeps its value.
  - [Camera] BaseZoomOut is now the closest the co-op camera gets to player 1, measured from player 1 (the author,
    review row 71: "Let's not overcomplicate it" / "set the default to where it currently is"). Before, it was added on
    top of the game's own camera distance. Default 800 (8 m = the game's usual ~5 m, from 399 log lines f1.169 -
    f1.197, + the old 3 m), allowed 100 - 3000 (was 0 - 3000); the app calls it "Distance back (m)"; presets Close
    5.5 m, Wide 9 m (were 0.5 / 4 m extra). An ini that says BaseZoomOut=300 now means 3 m - set it to 8 m.
  - [Camera] WallsOnly removed (the author: "should just be default on, not an option") - the co-op camera's wall
    check always stops only at walls and scenery (characters, enemies and props are looked past).
  - [General] DetailedLog removed - the detailed log is always on (the author, public beta: "won't we always want the
    detailed log?" / "Okay, do that."). g_DetailedLog starts true; the short-log filter (LogIsDetail) stays as the list
    of event / research lines for the end-of-project log clean-up. A DetailedLog line in an ini is ignored.
  - [Camera] ExtraTilt removed (the author: "Remove it.") - the co-op camera always looks 5 degrees steeper than the
    game's (CAM_EXTRA_TILT in core/02).
  - tools/test.sh: settings dumps differ from f1.199's only in the removed fields and the JoinAtStart wording.

f1.182 (2026-10-25) - a hit from player 2 or 3 starts the stagger, like player 1's (includes f1.181, untested).
  The author: "The issue isn't about building stagger, it is about triggering the stagger state. P3 was able to build the
  stagger bar all the way to Max, but it took a hit from P1 to actually trigger it." + "Stagger should only happen from
  human controlled players, NOT AI players".
  - Exe RE: the damage code 0x966EE0 adds the hit to the gauge (0x9672FC - 0x967321, clamped at max) and then starts the
    stagger (0x9673BA -> 0x967820, which calls the unit's vfunc +0x760) only if the hitter's unit +0x58 is the leader's
    type (0x967374 - 0x967383), or the hitter is on the players' side AND the attack's data [attack +0x40] +0x50 is set
    (some AI abilities). So an AI teammate - and players 2/3, who the game treats as AI - fill the gauge but can't finish it.
  - enemies/23: that leader check (17 bytes at 0x967374) goes through the same kind of stub as the v5.9 stagger-rate
    check (kStubBP): leader OR a type in the new g_MaskHuman (every type a person plays, no setting) -> 0x9673B0 (start
    the stagger); else 0x967385 (the game's AI rule). Log: "Patched stagger start leader check (0x967374)".

    manager (+0x60 = 0 while their window doesn't show -> their bars are cleared), +0xD8 = their target (menus/35b
    TargetCharOf; a weak reference is added on each change and never given back), then the game's tick on it. Its placing
    calls (0x13DBBC0 / 0x13DC010) get the point on player 1's camera ray that lands where their window shows the enemy
    (PipProject -> DeprojectScreenToWorld, at the distance from their window camera); not in their window = Collapsed.
    Player 1's target bar over a window = Collapsed. marker/19's drawn bar is skipped while the game's bar shows there.
  - Log: "GAMEBARS: player N's enemy status manager made ...", "player N's target <enemy> - K bar(s) in their manager, the
    game's bar placed in their window / not placed", "player 1's enemy bar over a window ... hidden there".
  - Risk HIGH (a new game object running the game's own bar code twice more per frame); GameEnemyBars=0 + F6 = the drawn
    bars (the second managers are then only kept hidden, not ticked). Research dumps cut to 6.

  would ultimately be the end goal". Decided: one borderless game window spanning the monitors; player 1 = the Windows
  main monitor ("Windows main monitor"), players 2 / 3 the others left to right; two monitors + three players = players 2
  and 3 side by side on the second ("vertical split sort of how we have set up for a wide screen monitor"; rows = an
  option). Plan: claude/FF7R_Multi_Monitor_Plan.md.
  - pip_hud/30c (new): MonitorTick (main tick, every 5 s) logs "MONITORS: N monitor(s): #1 x,y WxH (Windows main) ...",
    the game window + drawing area + style + canvas, and what the monitors layout WOULD give each player; once "MONITORS:
    game settings <class> - resolution, window mode; setters: ..." (UGameUserSettings via GetGameUserSettings, getters
    only - read-only ProcessEvent calls on the game thread). Always written (rare, max 20 lines). No behaviour change.
  - MonitorRegions(): monitors + drawing area + canvas -> each player's canvas rectangle (1 - 3 monitors, main first,
    others left to right, two-monitor sharing columns / rows, players not in, render scale, mixed heights).
    tools/tests/unit_monitors.cpp: 7 cases ALL OK. Not wired to the drawing yet (step 3).

    Python emulation again.

    now it waits like any change; (2) "in a battle" = InBattleNow() (the battle command menu) OR enemies the game lists
    for a battle (CollectEnemies every 250 ms - only while a party-size set is away from the game's normal): an ambush /

    the detailed log is switched on mid-game; device paths still in two rare INPUT2 SEATS lines (a seat set by USB port).

    log on (it walked every enemy's position when the count changed).

    the 6 game calls 5x a second) and the TALKDIAG lines; the talking-scene signal that stays = IsEnableFieldControler

    New line "TALKSCENE: talking-scene signal (field controls) found / NOT found".

f1.151b (2026-10-25) - F6 during a battle no longer changes the enemy settings (they wait until the battle is over).
  The author: "I am concerned that changing the enemy health values and things mid battle could cause the game to crash or
  have an issue ... Should we just disable that part of the settings update via F6 when a battle is currently going on?"
  -> "Yes. We should also add a note and then top of enemy AI settings that they changes cannot take affect during a
  battle." (Assessment given: crash risk low - the HP change uses the same game calls as at a battle's start, damage /
  attack rate are per-hit multipliers - but F6 could raise unscaled enemies' HP mid-fight, against the f1.151 guardrail.)
  - settings/03: g_BalBattle (= PartyBalanceTick's battle latch, incl. the 3 s end check) + g_BalReloadWaiting. LoadConfig
    applies the active set only outside a battle; in one it compares and, if anything changed, stores it - log "SETTINGS:
    reloaded during a battle - the changed enemy settings WAIT until the battle is over" / "... didn't change". [Balance]
    EnemiesTreatHumansLikePlayer1 follows the same rule (humanAggroFile -> humanAggro in ApplyBalanceSet).
  - enemies/21 PartyBalanceTick: once the battle is over "PARTYBAL: the enemy settings reloaded during the battle apply now".
  - core/26: the F6 notice says "Co-op settings reloaded - enemy settings apply after this battle" when they wait.
  - Everything else F6 reloads applies at once, as before.
  - Proof: settings dumps identical to f1.151 (all cases); a wine test with the real LoadConfig + PartyBalanceTick: F6 in
    the field = at once; in a battle = waits (also a 2nd F6), applies 3 s after the battle; changed-and-back = no change.
  - Settings app f1.151b: orange note at the top of the Balance tab ("Enemy settings never change during a battle ...");
    the party-size note names F6 too. check_log.py: the row includes the new SETTINGS line.

f1.151 (2026-10-25) - enemy settings per party size ([Balance1] / [Balance2] / [Balance3]).
  The author: "We need to delineate enemy settings under 3 drop down tabs... Single character party / Two character party /
  Three character party. We don't want a situation where when there is just a single party member the game is extremely
  difficult, but when there are 3 it is too easy." Decided: party size = characters in the party; the 2- and 3-character
  sets get "Only applies when human players are connected" (default on); every default = the game's normal; the mod
  follows party changes; GUARDRAIL: the values never change during a battle ("so the health of enemies doesn't change when
  a controller drops out and a player becomes AI"). This chat: 3 characters with 2 people playing = the 2-character set
  (the author: "2-character settings"); presets scale by party size (the author: "Scale by party size").
  - settings/03: BalanceSet (EnemyHP / EnemyDamage / EnemyAttackRate / StaggerRate / FocusOnPlayer2 / StayOnHitChance /
    TurnOnHeavyDamage / PreferWeakTargets / WeakTargetHealth + OnlyWithHumanPlayers), CoopConfig balSet[3] from [Balance1]
    / [Balance2] / [Balance3]; the old enemy keys in [Balance] are no longer read ([Balance] keeps the per-player ATB /
    menu slowdown / EnemiesTreatHumansLikePlayer1). The active values (balHP ... balWeakHealth) = a copy of the active set
    (ApplyBalanceSet, g_BalSetIdx); F6 re-copies the active set's new values. Default ini: the three sections.
  - enemies/21 PartyBalanceTick (from EnemyBalanceTick, game thread, not while paused): set = PartyCount(); with
    OnlyWithHumanPlayers the number of people playing (player 1 + players 2/3 whose movement pass runs) caps it. Outside
    battles: switches once the party looked the same for 1 s. In a battle (InBattleNow): the switch WAITS; the battle
    counts as over after 3 s without InBattleNow (count restarts after a pause / loading gap and during cutscenes).
    Log: "PARTYBAL: party of N, N people playing -> the N-character enemy settings ...", "... WAIT until this battle is
    over", "PARTYBAL: battle - ... stay until it is over", "PARTYBAL: battle over", "... (was N-character, waited for the
    battle to end)". Simulated (stub harness): joins in the field, a drop-out mid-battle, wave breaks, a 10 s pause, a
    battle-end cutscene, a change 0.5 s before a battle, back-and-forth in a battle - all as intended.
  - The co-op-only checks are gone from the EnemyDamage / EnemyAttackRate hooks and EnemyBalanceTick (the party-size set
    decides; with the game's normal values nothing changes, as before). With player 1 alone the targeting's enemy list
    isn't refreshed, so BalEnemyList asks the game itself (CollectEnemies every 250 ms) - only while an active value is
    away from the game's normal. FocusOnPlayer2 still needs player 2 / 3.
  - tools: unit_settings dumps the three sets + g_BalSetIdx; settings_cases/partysets.ini (odd values, out of range, a
    missing key); settings proof: the old cases differ only where their old [Balance] enemy keys are no longer read;
    The author's ini (moved to the new sections, same values) = identical active values. check_log.py: the PARTYBAL row.
    make_presets.py: (section, key) values + fixed (it broke on the DEFAULT_INI_PLAYER_COMMON macro).
  - Settings app f1.151: Balance tab > "Enemy settings per party size" with a drop-down (Single / Two / Three character
    party) showing that set's Enemy toughness + Who enemies go after (+ the OnlyWithHumanPlayers box for two / three);
    "Who enemies go after (every party size)" keeps EnemiesTreatHumansLikePlayer1. Difficulty presets fill all three
    (Balanced: 1 = normal, 2 = HP 125 / stagger 90 / damage 105, 3 = the old values; Hard / Extreme: 1 a little tougher, 2
    about halfway - Claude's proposal, to be tuned); preset files save [Balance] + [Balance1-3].
  - His ini: the [Balance] enemy keys (all at the game's normal) moved to [Balance1] / [Balance2] / [Balance3].

    The rows' unmoved places are kept RELATIVE TO THE OWNER'S ROW (never moved by us) -> a moving panel doesn't matter.
    Slot units -> pixels with the panel's scale (HcRect of Status_C; f1.140 used the row's scale: 74 px for 67), then
    calibrated from the measured move (kept within 0.5x - 2x). A row we moved is never picked as the owner's row (lowest
    row). Lines: "party row slot SetPosition found", "row Status_0N: moving it X px down over the hidden row(s) (Top a ->
    b)", "row Status_0N moved X px (wanted Y) - gap closed / did NOT move - gap NOT closed / moved by a different amount -
    recalibrating", "pixels per unit a -> b (measured)", "back in its place (no gap to close)", "the game moved the row
    itself ...".
  - Removed: [PictureInPicture] PartyPanelCloseGaps (settings/03 field + default ini lines + SETTINGS line; unit_settings
    print), try 2 (root widget translation), settings app f1.141: the Experimental "Health / ATB panel" box and f1.138b's
    orange known-issue note. Settings proof: only the removed field / lines differ. README draft: the gap known issue gone.

f1.140 (2026-10-25) - EXPERIMENTAL gap fix, second try: two other ways, PartyPanelCloseGaps = 0 off / 1 try 1 / 2 try 2.
  The author: "We can try them. If they fail, then agreed, it's not the end of the world."
  - RE (object dump + exe): Status_C's rows Status_00 / 01 / 02 sit in an EndCanvasPanel (WidgetTree.All_Status, one
    EndCanvasPanelSlot each - the game's own slot class with XAngle / YAngle tilt). Status_In has only colour tracks (it
    doesn't move rows). The rows' own anims (BattleField_Shift / Shift2 / CommandOpen / MemberJoin / MemberLeft) animate
    2D transforms / colours / margins of widgets INSIDE the row. Guess (not proven): the EndCanvasPanel ignores its
    children's render transforms, which is why f1.139's row translation never showed.

    the Slate slot (+0x70). UWidget RenderTransform +0x90 (Translation +0x0), Slot +0x28; UUserWidget WidgetTree +0x198;
    WidgetTree RootWidget +0x28.

    EndCanvasPanelSlot); try 2 = the row's root widget's render translation Y + shift. Bookkeeping fixed: the plan uses the

    screen (wanted Y) - gap fix try N WORKS / did NOT move it / moved it by a different amount". Other lines: "gap fix try

  leader auto switch thing be disabled when there are no AI characters available? I assume that is ultimately the cause."
  - RE: the KO auto-switch = the unit's KO-state handler 0x9864C0 (unit+0x611 == 0xD; vtable entries 0x4B655C0 ...): if
    the downed unit's type (+0x58) is the leader type (party mgr +0x233) it calls the switch routine 0x9BAA80(pc global
    0x57E9720, dl 1, r8b 1, r9b 0) at 0x98660E and jumps to the end - the rest of its KO handling (0x98661F on: KO sub-state
    unit+0x2DC, ...) only runs for a downed NON-leader. The routine picks via 0x9CC4C0 (Hook_CanSwitchTo).
  - seats/13 Hook_CanSwitchTo: with KoKeepLeader=1, the "player 1 down + no AI teammate left" case returns 0 for players
    2/3 too -> nobody is picked, player 1's character stays the leader, players 2/3 keep their own systems / menus.

    hate 1000, everyone else at most 400, re-score flag ctrl+0x1A60 -> it turns before its next attack. No roll for the
    switch it caused or while it is already after them. Only the people playing (not KO'd). Was f1.47/48's weight in the
    dead SetTargetByType path (it never did anything). Checked 10 times a second in battle (enemies/22 WeakTargetTick).
  - NEW [Balance] WeakTargetHealth (1-99 %, default 50): below this share of max health a player counts as weakened.

    remembered for 5 s (f1.92 logged up to 5 lines for one turn).

f1.92 (2026-10-22) - BUG-3 fixed: who enemies go after, as hate-score rules (the author: "Let's go with option 2. Leave
  default at the normal values, but allow for a change. We can crank one up really high for testing").
  f1.91 TARGETLOG (14:29-14:32, his usual fight, alone): real rates target -200, others +200 (+0-100) / AI teammates +50;
  scores cap at 1000, so enemies change target after almost every attack; shares P1 37 / P2 37 / P3 23 / AI 3 %. No hate
  from damage. Details: claude/FF7R_Enemy_Targeting_RE.md section 5.
  - enemies/22_attention.inc rewritten. Gone (approved with option 2): the SetTargetByType thunk hook (never called),
    AttnChoose / AttnRoll / AttnApplyNow, [Balance] ShareEnemyAttention (DLL, default ini, app, presets), ATTENTION DIAG.
  - [Balance] StayOnHitChance (default now 0 = the game's normal; was 75 for the dead path): when an enemy lands a hit on
    the player it is after (OnGiveDamage, damage > 0, not dodged / perfect-guarded, InTarget = its current target), with
    that chance that player's hate goes to 1000 and everyone else's to at most 400 - after the game's re-score (800 vs at
    most 700) it stays on them. A miss changes nothing, so the game's rotation moves it on.
  - NEW [Balance] TurnOnHeavyDamage (% of the enemy's max HP, default 0 = off): a player who deals that much damage within a
    few seconds (half-life 4 s) and more than 1.5x what the enemy's current target dealt gets hate 1000, everyone else at
    most 400, and the game's re-score flag ctrl+0x1A60 is set -> it turns at its next re-score.
  - Only the people playing are pushed up (leader + joined players 2/3, not KO'd); AI teammates are left to the game.
  - Log: "ATTENTION: <enemy> hit <player> ... stays on them", "ATTENTION: <enemy> turns on <player> ...", and every 30 s
    "ATTENTION: last 30 s - enemies landed N hits on the player they were after (M made them stay) ... heavy-damage turns".
    TARGETLOG (f1.91) stays on to see the effect.
  - Settings app: Balance tab "Who enemies go after" - ShareEnemyAttention row gone, StayOnHitChance default 0, new
    TurnOnHeavyDamage row, note text; presets: "Default" StayOnHitChance 0, TurnOnHeavyDamage 0 in all four (the Co-op
    presets keep 75 / 80 / 90 - now they really make enemies stay). Headless-checked (rows, save, screenshot).
  - Found, NOT changed (the author to decide): PreferWeakTargets only ever worked through the dead SetTargetByType path, so
    it has never done anything (the "Co-op Extreme" preset uses it).

f1.91 (2026-10-22) - log only: how enemies pick their targets (BUG-3 step A; the author: "Okay, do that" - tested on his
  usual fight). RE: claude/FF7R_Enemy_Targeting_RE.md - enemies never call SetTargetByType; each enemy AI controller keeps
  a hate score per party member (ctrl+0x1AC8, 0..1000), re-scored after each of its actions (0x93ED90: the target goes
  down, everyone else up, + random) and the highest score is the target (0x93FE20).
  - NEW enemies/22b_target_log.inc (called from EnemyBalanceTick, game thread, 10 times a second in battle):
    TARGETLOG lines for every enemy target change (enemy -> character (P1 / P2 / P3 / AI), was ... for N s | everyone's
    hate score), capped at 600 a session; every 30 s and at each battle's end the share of time enemies spent on P1 / P2 /
    P3 / AI teammates; once per battle the real hate rates (the game's own parameter lookup 0xB7A500 on the table at

    party change / swap = not known or another character).

    order; via another page if it already sits on that page) - once per wanted character / party order. Log "PAGES: player
    N's own menu quietly set up on <character> (page X)".

    used SetPartyLeader only, which moves the leader but not the camera / battle input (the hand-off itself went through the
    switch routine). P2LeadTick now calls 0x9BAA80(*(base+0x57E9720), 1, 1, 1) - the global both game callers pass (RE:
    0x98660E KO auto-switch, 0x141D98E D-pad), r8b 1 = forced, r9b 1 = now or never (0 would queue a pending switch that the
    game runs later without our force) - with g_SwitchForce = player 1's character, so Hook_CanSwitchTo lets only that one
    pass; Hook_SetLeader ends the hand-off. If the leader didn't change: the old SetPartyLeader way. Log "LEADER: lead handed
    back to player 1 (the game's switch - camera and controls move too)" or "(SetPartyLeader only ...)".

    with ";" between entries and only the first entry was used (fresh installs; only the mod's HUD-copy fallback uses them).
  - [General] DirectInputPads=Off is ignored (log NOTE): the game's own DirectInput / PlayStation readers always get neutral
    input. Off would have let players 2/3's DirectInput / PlayStation controllers drive player 1 too. (The v9.9 reload block
    for DirectInputPads in core/26 can't trigger any more - removal checklist B.)

    colours, player 1's menu keys / players 2-3's menu buttons say when they are used, one JoinWithController note.

  - Settings app f1.22: the Player 3 tab's F6 note only shows when the installed mod is older than f1.22.
  - Code review fixes (claude/FF7R_Code_Review_f1_22.md, picked by the author 2026-10-19):
    C1 OwnCommandMenu=2: while a player's own command menu is open, D-pad up/down no longer also switches their character
       and their sticks don't move / aim (OwnCmdMenuOpen, menus/10; PollPad2, P2SwitchTick).
    C2 the AI controller a person took over is kept as a weak reference: after a save load / chapter change the hand-back
       doesn't write into freed memory any more (log: "the AI controller ... is gone - nothing to hand back there").
    C4 lead keys are refused during a KO hand-off (on-screen note) - before, the wrong person drove the leading character.
    B2 the game's rumble stops on the old leader's controller at a hot swap / when the leader goes to keyboard or DirectInput.
    A1 F6 re-makes the split-screen windows when FullSizeHud, FullSizeHudQuality, TemporalAA or FOV changed.
    A3 ReloadKey / HelpKey written with Ctrl/Shift/Alt: the key is used without them (NOTE) instead of being ignored.
    A4 join, co-op camera and reload keys need exactly their own Ctrl/Shift/Alt (HotkeyModsExact), like the lead keys.
    C5 a shared-menu turn ending with the battle / a leave no longer freezes the hold-off (palette blocked next battle);
       fresh action / request copies each battle.
    App: a button set to None stays None (was shown as the default), ReloadKey / HelpKey recorded without modifiers, Esc
       cancels a hotkey recording (menu keys can still be Escape), warning on the General tab when two mod actions share a key.
    Tested: build (11 known warnings), settings read under wine (ReloadKey=Ctrl+F4 -> F4 + NOTE), app headless (unchanged save

  default on; 0 = the game's own rule only). Asked by the author. RE: claude/FF7R_Shared_Items_RE.md (subagent, spot-checked).
  - The game reserves an item only when the action STARTS (dispatch 0x141B1C0) and takes it when the effect lands; queued

    (0x141ABD0 hooked as a guard: the dispatching command must not count against itself), only in battles (InBattleNow:
    outside battles some game code writes GetItemNum's answer back to the inventory), only own command systems that are on.

f1.20 (2026-10-19) - two command menus at once, step B (claude/FF7R_Two_Menus_RE.md 1.3 / 3.3): per-player focus contexts,
  only with [General] OwnCommandMenu=2 (read at start; 0 = off, the default and unchanged; 1 = the old v16.6-v18.3 way). Includes
  f1.19's FOCUS log (now only in battles). Reviewed twice by a subagent against the exe (no crash / reference / signature bugs left).
  - The game's focus manager (*(base+0x59CA980)) has ONE focus path / stack / key-capture set; every Command_C pushes itself at
    battle setup, so the newest (player 2/3's) owned focus. Now the path (+0x08), stack (+0x40), captures (+0x98) and +0x188 are
    kept per player: player 1's stay in the object, players 2/3's are swapped in (raw bytes = ownership moves) only while their
    code runs: a push / pop / SetFocus / SetFocusToChild on a widget inside their Command_C (owner test = GetCachedWidget +
    IsDescendantOrSelf), their own command system's update / SetBattle, and the key events made from their pad.
  - CtxKeys (replaces Menu2Keys in mode 2): while their own command menu is open (ui >= 9) and their focus path leads into their
    own Command_C, their D-pad (with repeats) / A / B become FKeyEvents for the game's UI processor KeyDown 0x1436720 / KeyUp
    0x1438280 inside their context (last-input flags +0x52..+0x6B kept player 1's). Keys still held get their key-up when the
    menu closes, their pass is skipped or their command system goes off. Bcm2MenuKey then gives their command system the game's
    own answer for D-pad / A / B (their Command_C's key table, filled by those events, like player 1's) instead of the pad.
  - Player 1's real key events and the processor Tick are forced into player 1's context (vtable 0x4BF24B0 +0x08 / +0x10 / +0x18
    hooked; also captures the FSlateApplication). Command_C key forwarding (v17.1) leaves a player's own events alone while their
    context is in. The v18.3 dispatcher cut / claims / UiDump are off in mode 2. If any focus hook fails: mode 2 switches off (log).
  - Logs: `CTX: per-player focus contexts ON ...`, `CTX: player N's own command menu now takes / no longer takes their pad (...)`,
    `CTX: player N key <FKey> down/repeat/up | their path a -> b, leaf Pn | player 1's path unchanged / CHANGED`, FOCUS lines say
    `in Pn's context (switched)`.

f1.10 (2026-10-18) - DualSense over Bluetooth (test 8: "buttons do wrong actions, movement laggy"). A Bluetooth
  DualSense / DualShock 4 starts in "simple" mode (short report 0x01 in the DS4 layout, sent less often) until the
  host reads its calibration feature report. f1.9 decoded the DualSense's simple report with the USB layout (face
  buttons came from the L2 byte, etc.) and relied on the game to switch it to full reports. Now the new module's HID
  reader asks for full reports itself (feature 0x05 DualSense / 0x02 DS4, again every 2 s up to 5 times while simple
  reports keep coming), decodes Bluetooth simple reports with the right layout, and keeps a PlayStation pad's last
  report while its reader runs (Bluetooth can send only on change - before, its state counted as stale after 0.5-1 s
  and the read fell back to DirectInput, which gets nothing from a DualSense the game holds). Logs the report kind
  ("Bluetooth simple / full / USB"). [input2/in2.cpp] xinput1_3_f1_10.dll md5 12c991ee95ad310a759339c2bab01b75.

  which controller each seat has, the old code follows. Each seat is tied once, at the start, to the controller the
  old code picked from the ini ([PlayerN] settings) - or [Input2] SeatN - and keeps that physical controller by
  identity. When Windows gives it another XInput slot after an unplug / re-plug, the old code's controller code is
  moved to it (INPUT2 SEATS "the old code now uses ..."); a seat whose own controller is missing and still holds that
  code is moved off it (leader -> keyboard). While a seat's controller is unplugged its reads report "not connected",
  whatever sits on its old XInput slot (leader: game slot 0 idle). Hot swaps trade the seats in the seats module too.
  The old ControllerSlot=Auto scan stops for a seat once it has its controller. Known limit: a DirectInput pad must
  still be named in [PlayerN] DirectInputController (step 2.2); a re-plugged XInput pad needs one press before its
  slot is matched (until then the old reading of its old slot is used). Reviewed twice; fixes in. Without Seats=New:
  as f1.5. xinput1_3_f1_6.dll md5 b9876a53a4d00e5adc66049dfd86c198.

f1.5 (2026-10-18) - fix attempt for "Barret (P2) stuck firing after a battle" (seen 4 times; every time an attack in the
  last ~1 s of the battle, on player 2's own command system). When the battle ends, that system runs on for ~1 s
  (flicker guard) with the player's live buttons, then stops being updated - a held attack stayed held in it. Now,
  from the 3rd update of that window, it gets no buttons, so the hold is released there before it goes off. Logs
  "CMD2: player N's battle is ending ...". 7 lines in menus/10_second_command_system.inc, 1 in passes/12_command_passes.inc.

f1.4 (2026-10-18) - (1) [Input2] Pads=New: every read the mod makes of a player's controller (PadGet: players 2/3's
  pads, the command menu / shortcut palette, the menu pass-through, slot 0) comes from the new devices module, each
  seat's controller kept by identity (seats module, tied to the old code's choice every frame, so hot swaps follow
  at once). Identity only counts while that controller is still where the old code reads it (same XInput slot /
  DirectInput GUID) - the old code's rumble, hiding and menus use that code; if it moved, it is logged and the old
  code's controller there is read. Old reading still runs first and is the fallback (no fresh state < 150 ms,
  refused / unopened DirectInput pad). DirectInput pads through the old code's button map. Own packet counter per
  controller. Logs INPUT2 PADS (source changes, 30 s summary). Seats module now locked (SRW) between threads.
  (2) GameInput=XInput is now the DEFAULT (GameInput=Game = the old behaviour). REMOVED: the v19.5-v19.8
  DirectInput pass-through of the leader's controller and the v19.8 synthesised Xbox reading (39_dinput_hide.inc,
  ~55 lines): every controller the game opens through DirectInput is now always idle. If the XInput patch can't be
  applied (or GameInput=Game) and the game goes into DirectInput mode, a WARNING is logged - the leader then has no
  controller in the game. Reviewed; fixes from the review in. 1 line added to the old code (PadGet).
  xinput1_3_f1_4.dll md5 c7887c7443c7a6004aac4c7df5769f1e. Installed for test 5.

f1.3 (2026-10-18) - XInput slot 0 (the leader's controller) can be built by the new devices module: [Input2] Slot0=New
  (needs Mode=Watch; meant together with GameInput=XInput). Which controller still follows the old code (hot swap);

f1.2 (2026-10-18) - leader output channel "XInput only", OFF by default ([Input2] GameInput=XInput turns it on).
  RE (static, ff7remake_.exe): the game reads gamepads in XInput mode OR DirectInput mode (global flag RVA 0x57E9A0C);
  in DirectInput mode it reads only the DirectInput controller saved in its settings (or the first one it finds when
  no XInput pad is attached) and ignores XInput. Details: Claude project doc FF7R_Mod_Reference.md section 5.5.
  input2\output: patches the game's DirectInput device vtable (FEndDirectInput, RVA 0x4C29EB0): Select /
  SelectProduct refuse, Tick undoes an earlier pick (the game's own DeselectAll + flag back to XInput). All slots are
  checked first; on any mismatch nothing is patched. Then XInput slot 0 (the leader seat, built by the old code) is the
  only controller the game reads. Glue: In2Start reads [Input2] GameInput (independent of Mode).
  Test: tools\tests\unit_output.cpp (fake vtable, wine) passes. xinput1_3_f1_2.dll md5 4468bda36a2d7acadc3edb634dadc33d. Not installed.
